<script data-cookieconsent="ignore">
(function () {
    if (!/Instagram|FBAN|FBAV|FB_IAB/.test(navigator.userAgent)) { return; }
    if (window.location.hash) { return; }

    var restoreScrollRestoration = false;
    try {
        if ('scrollRestoration' in history && history.scrollRestoration === 'auto') {
            history.scrollRestoration = 'manual';
            restoreScrollRestoration = true;
        }
    } catch (e) {}

    var armedUntil = Date.now() + 15000;
    var maxCorrections = 6;
    var corrections = 0;
    var lastY = window.scrollY || 0;
    var lastT = Date.now();
    var userTouching = false;
    var touchedAt = 0;

    function onGestureStart(e) { if (e.isTrusted) { userTouching = true; touchedAt = Date.now(); } }
    function onGestureEnd(e) { if (e.isTrusted) { touchedAt = Date.now(); userTouching = false; } }
    function onGesture(e) { if (e.isTrusted) { touchedAt = Date.now(); } }

    window.addEventListener('touchstart', onGestureStart, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchend', onGestureEnd, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchcancel', onGestureEnd, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onGesture, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onGesture, { capture: true, passive: true });

    function tick() {
        var now = Date.now();
        var y = window.scrollY;
        var dt = now - lastT;
        var dy = y - lastY;

        var jumpThreshold = Math.max(window.innerHeight * 1.5, 800);
        var recentGesture = userTouching || (now - touchedAt < 1000);

        if (dy > jumpThreshold && dt < 250 && !recentGesture && corrections < maxCorrections) {
            window.scrollTo(0, lastY);
            y = lastY;
            corrections++;
        }

        lastY = y;
        lastT = now;

        if (now < armedUntil) {
            requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        } else if (restoreScrollRestoration) {
            try { history.scrollRestoration = 'auto'; } catch (e) {}
        }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
})();
</script>

font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; flex: 1 1 auto">„Christian ist ein wirklich guter Berater. Er half mir bei der korrekten und detaillierten Marktanalyse sowie mit Empfehlungen, wie ich in Bezug auf meine Geschäftsziele vorgehen soll.“</blockquote><figcaption style="font-size: 14px; display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; border-top: 1px solid #EFEFEF; padding-top: 14px"><img src="https://onecdn.io/media/1f3a5cb9-6721-4de7-87ee-1a34c5dd5913/sm" alt="Atanas Georgiev Rusev" width="40" height="40" loading="lazy" style="flex: 0 0 40px; width: 40px; height: 40px; border-radius: 50%; object-fit: cover; border: 2px solid #F6C3DA"><span><strong>Atanas Georgiev Rusev</strong><br><span style="color: #6B6B6B">Geschäftsführer</span></span></figcaption></figure><figure style="margin: 0; background: #FFFFFF; color: #191919; border-radius: 18px; box-shadow: 0 18px 40px rgba(90,0,35,0.28); padding: 26px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px"><svg width="30" height="24" viewBox="0 0 30 24" fill="#D2126C" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 24V14C0 6 4 1.5 12 0l1.5 3.5C9 5 7 8 7 11h6v13zm16 0V14c0-8 4-12.5 12-14l1.5 3.5C25 5 23 8 23 11h6v13z"></path></svg><blockquote style="margin: 0; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6; flex: 1 1 auto">„Ich kann Christian voller Überzeugung weiterempfehlen! Wenn ich die Zusammenarbeit in drei Worten beschreiben müsste, wären das: erfolgreich, hilfreich und nett.“</blockquote><figcaption style="font-size: 14px; display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center; border-top: 1px solid #EFEFEF; padding-top: 14px"><span style="flex: 0 0 40px; height: 40px; border-radius: 50%; background: #FFF0F6; border: 2px solid #F6C3DA; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #A50E55">MB</span><span><strong>Marie Blitzner</strong><br><span style="color: #6B6B6B">Head of Recruiting</span></span></figcaption></figure></div></div></section><section style="max-width: 860px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 72px 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px"><h2 style="margin: 0 0 8px; font-family: 'Inter Tight', sans-serif; font-weight: 800; font-size: 38px">Häufige Fragen</h2><details style="border-bottom: 1px solid #E4E4E4; padding: 16px 0"><summary style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer">Was kostet das?</summary><p style="margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.6; color: #3A3A3A">Die Marktanalyse kostet nichts. Ein Honorar fällt erst an, wenn Sie jemanden über uns einstellen. Es beträgt 20 % des Bruttojahresgehalts. Die Bedingungen bekommen Sie mit der Auftragsbestätigung.</p></details><details style="border-bottom: 1px solid #E4E4E4; padding: 16px 0"><summary style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer">Muss ich nach der Analyse einen Auftrag geben?</summary><p style="margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.6; color: #3A3A3A">Nein. Wir suchen erst, wenn Sie uns ausdrücklich beauftragen.</p></details><details style="border-bottom: 1px solid #E4E4E4; padding: 16px 0"><summary style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer">Wer spricht mit den Kandidaten?</summary><p style="margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.6; color: #3A3A3A">Ein Recruiter aus unserem Team. Die Suchagenten finden die Profile, das Gespräch führt immer ein Mensch.</p></details><details style="border-bottom: 1px solid #E4E4E4; padding: 16px 0"><summary style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer">Was passiert mit meinen Angaben?</summary><p style="margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.6; color: #3A3A3A">Wir brauchen sie für die Marktanalyse und um Ihre Anfrage zu bearbeiten. Details stehen im <a href="/impressum">Datenschutzhinweis</a>.</p></details><details style="border-bottom: 1px solid #E4E4E4; padding: 16px 0"><summary style="font-size: 17px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer">Welche Stellen übernehmen Sie nicht?</summary><p style="margin: 12px 0 0; font-size: 16px; line-height: 1.6; color: #3A3A3A">Helferjobs und gewerbliche Stellen, zum Beispiel im Lager, in der Produktion oder im Fahrdienst. Wir suchen Fach- und Führungskräfte mit Ausbildung oder Studium.</p></details></section><section style="max-width: 1160px; margin: 0 auto 72px; padding: 0 24px"><div style="border-radius: 18px; background: #FFF0F6; border: 1px solid #F6C3DA; color: #191919; padding: 48px 40px; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 24px; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between"><div style="flex: 1 1 380px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px"><h2 style="margin: 0; font-family: 'Inter Tight', sans-serif; font-weight: 800; font-size: 32px">Probieren Sie es mit Ihrer nächsten Stelle.</h2><p style="margin: 0; font-size: 17px; color: #3A3A3A">Die Marktanalyse ist kostenlos. Ob wir danach suchen, entscheiden Sie.</p></div><a href="#chat" style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; min-height: 48px; padding: 0 24px; border-radius: 4px; background: radial-gradient(100% 100% at 100% 0, #EE3934 0, #D2126C 100%); color: #FFFFFF; font-family: 'Inter Tight', sans-serif; font-weight: 700; font-size: 16px; text-decoration: none">Stelle jetzt eingeben</a></div></section></div>
            <script>
                (function () {
            var run = function () {
                (function(){
  var AGENT = 'agent_4001m63cmvg4fq0vdsq5j175mts7';
  var LIB = 'https://unpkg.com/@elevenlabs/client@1.28.0/dist/lib.iife.js';
  var GREETING = 'Hallo! Welche Stelle ist bei Ihnen offen? Titel und Ort reichen für den Anfang. Sie können auch eine Stellenanzeige als PDF oder Link schicken.';
  var KEY = 'pycruit_hinweis_ok';

  var p = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  var f = (p.get('firma') || '').replace(/[<>]/g, '').slice(0, 80).trim();
  var g = document.getElementById('pc-gruss');
  if (f && g) { document.getElementById('pc-firma').textContent = f; g.style.display = 'block'; }

  if (!document.getElementById('pc-fonts')) {
    var l = document.createElement('link');
    l.id = 'pc-fonts'; l.rel = 'stylesheet';
    l.href = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600&family=Inter+Tight:wght@700;800&display=swap';
    document.head.appendChild(l);
  }

  // Alles, was von der Seite wegführt, öffnet sich in einem neuen Tab. So bleibt der Chat offen.
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('a[href]') : null;
    if (!a) return;
    var h = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
    if (/^(#|tel:|mailto:|javascript:)/i.test(h)) return;
    a.target = '_blank'; a.rel = 'noopener';
  }, true);

  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var log = $('pcc-log'), input = $('pcc-in'), form = $('pcc-form'), send = $('pcc-send');
  var chips = $('pcc-chips'), consent = $('pcc-consent'), fileIn = $('pcc-file'), status = $('pcc-status');
  var vpanel = $('pcc-voice'), vstat = $('pcc-vstat');
  if (!log || log.getAttribute('data-ready')) return;
  log.setAttribute('data-ready', '1');

  var conv = null, starting = null, skipFirst = false, ended = false, dots = null, sid = 0;

  function esc(t) { return String(t).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;'); }
  function md(t) {
    var h = esc(String(t).trim());
    h = h.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>');
    h = h.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
    h = h.replace(/(^|[\s(])(https?:\/\/[^\s<)]+)/g, '$1<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$2</a>');
    return h.split(/\n{2,}/).map(function (x) { return '<p>' + x.replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</p>'; }).join('');
  }
  function scroll() { log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight; }
  function add(cls, html) {
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'pcc-m ' + cls; d.innerHTML = html;
    if (dots && dots.parentNode) log.insertBefore(d, dots); else log.appendChild(d);
    scroll(); return d;
  }
  function typing(on) {
    if (on && !dots) { dots = document.createElement('div'); dots.className = 'pcc-dots'; dots.innerHTML = '<i></i><i></i><i></i>'; log.appendChild(dots); scroll(); }
    if (!on && dots) { dots.remove(); dots = null; }
  }
  function setChips(list) {
    chips.innerHTML = '';
    (list || []).forEach(function (t) {
      var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = t;
      b.onclick = function () { chips.innerHTML = ''; say(t); };
      chips.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  function grow() { input.style.height = 'auto'; input.style.height = Math.min(input.scrollHeight, 160) + 'px'; }
  function okStored() { try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY) === '1'; } catch (e) { return false; } }

  function loadLib() {
    if (window.ElevenLabsClient) return Promise.resolve();
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var s = document.createElement('script'); s.src = LIB; s.async = true;
      s.onload = res; s.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('lib')); };
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    });
  }

  function onAgent(text) {
    if (skipFirst) { skipFirst = false; if (text.trim() === GREETING) return; }
    typing(false);
    add('ai', md(text));
    if (/passt das so\??\s*$/i.test(text.trim())) setChips(['Ja, das passt so', 'Ich möchte etwas ändern']);
  }

  function history() {
    var out = [], me = 0;
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(log.querySelectorAll('.pcc-m.ai, .pcc-m.me'), function (el) {
      var who = el.classList.contains('me') ? 'Kunde' : 'Agent';
      if (who === 'Kunde') me++;
      out.push(who + ': ' + el.textContent.trim());
    });
    if (!me) return '';
    return out.join('\n').slice(-6000);
  }

  function start(voice, hist) {
    if (conv) return Promise.resolve(conv);
    if (starting) return starting;
    ended = false; skipFirst = true;
    var my = ++sid;
    status.textContent = 'verbindet …';
    starting = loadLib().then(function () {
      return window.ElevenLabsClient.Conversation.startSession({
        agentId: AGENT,
        textOnly: !voice,
        dynamicVariables: { firma: f || 'Ihr Unternehmen' },
        onConnect: function () { if (my !== sid) return; status.textContent = voice ? 'von pycruit · Sprachmodus' : 'von pycruit · online'; },
        onMessage: function (m) {
          if (my !== sid) return;
          var role = m.role || m.source;
          if (!m.message) return;
          if (role === 'user') { if (voice) add('me', md(m.message)); return; }
          onAgent(m.message);
        },
        onModeChange: function (x) {
          if (!voice || my !== sid) return;
          var talk = x && x.mode === 'speaking';
          vpanel.classList.toggle('talk', talk);
          vstat.textContent = talk ? 'Der Agent spricht …' : 'Ich höre zu. Sprechen Sie einfach.';
        },
        onError: function () { typing(false); },
        onDisconnect: function () {
          if (my !== sid) return;
          conv = null; starting = null; typing(false); status.textContent = 'von pycruit';
          if (voice) showVoice(false);
          if (!ended) { ended = true; add('sys', 'Das Gespräch ist beendet. Schreiben Sie einfach weiter, um eine weitere Stelle einzugeben.'); }
        }
      });
    }).then(function (c) {
      if (my !== sid) { try { c.endSession(); } catch (e) {} return c; }
      conv = c; starting = null;
      if (hist) { try { c.sendContextualUpdate('Bisheriger Verlauf dieses Gesprächs. Knüpfe daran an und frage nichts erneut, was schon beantwortet ist:\n' + hist); } catch (e) {} }
      return c;
    }, function (e) {
      starting = null; typing(false); status.textContent = 'von pycruit';
      if (voice) { showVoice(false); add('sys', 'Ohne Mikrofon klappt der Sprachmodus nicht. Sie können einfach weiter tippen.'); }
      else add('sys', 'Der Chat ist gerade nicht erreichbar. Bitte versuchen Sie es gleich noch einmal oder rufen Sie uns an: +49 162 26 77 665.');
      throw e;
    });
    return starting;
  }

  function showVoice(on) {
    vpanel.style.display = on ? 'flex' : 'none';
    form.style.display = on ? 'none' : 'flex';
    vpanel.classList.remove('talk');
    vstat.textContent = 'Verbinde …';
    if (!on) setTimeout(function () { input.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 50);
  }

  function startVoice() {
    setChips([]);
    var hist = history();
    var prev = conv;
    sid++; conv = null; starting = null; typing(false);
    showVoice(true);
    var go = function () { return start(true, hist).catch(function () {}); };
    if (prev) Promise.resolve(prev.endSession()).catch(function () {}).then(go);
    else go();
  }

  function stopVoice() {
    ended = true; sid++;
    var c = conv; conv = null; starting = null;
    showVoice(false);
    status.textContent = 'von pycruit';
    if (c) Promise.resolve(c.endSession()).catch(function () {});
  }

  function say(text) {
    text = String(text || '').trim();
    if (!text) return;
    setChips([]);
    input.placeholder = 'Antwort schreiben …';
    var hist = conv ? '' : history();
    add('me', md(text));
    typing(true);
    start(false, hist).then(function (c) { c.sendUserMessage(text); }).catch(function () {});
  }

  form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var t = input.value; input.value = ''; grow(); say(t);
  });
  input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.isComposing) { e.preventDefault(); form.requestSubmit ? form.requestSubmit() : form.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit')); }
  });
  input.addEventListener('input', function () { grow(); if (conv) try { conv.sendUserActivity(); } catch (e) {} });

  $('pcc-attach').addEventListener('click', function () { fileIn.click(); });
  $('pcc-mic').addEventListener('click', function () { if (!input.disabled) startVoice(); });
  $('pcc-vend').addEventListener('click', stopVoice);
  fileIn.addEventListener('change', function () {
    var file = fileIn.files && fileIn.files[0]; fileIn.value = '';
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) { add('sys', 'Die Datei ist größer als 10 MB. Schicken Sie gern einen Link zur Anzeige.'); return; }
    var text = input.value.trim(); input.value = ''; grow();
    setChips([]);
    var hist = conv ? '' : history();
    add('me', '<span class="pcc-file">Datei: ' + esc(file.name) + '</span>' + (text ? md(text) : ''));
    typing(true);
    start(false, hist).then(function (c) {
      return c.uploadFile(file).then(function (r) {
        c.sendMultimodalMessage({ text: text || 'Hier ist unsere Stellenanzeige.', fileIds: [r.fileId] });
      });
    }).catch(function () { typing(false); add('sys', 'Die Datei konnte nicht hochgeladen werden. Schicken Sie gern einen Link zur Anzeige.'); });
  });

  function unlock() {
    consent.style.display = 'none';
    input.disabled = false; send.disabled = false;
    if (!log.children.length) add('ai', md(GREETING));
  }
  if (okStored()) unlock();
  else { input.disabled = true; send.disabled = true; }
  $('pcc-ok').addEventListener('click', function () {
    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, '1'); } catch (e) {}
    unlock(); input.focus(); loadLib().catch(function () {});
  });

  document.querySelectorAll('#pc-root a[href="#chat"]').forEach(function (a) {
    a.addEventListener('click', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!input.disabled) input.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, 400); });
  });
})();

            };
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', run);
            } else {
                run();
            }
        })()
            </script>

function processOneEffects() {
    if (!window.oneEffects) { return }
    for(var i = 0; i < window.oneEffects.length; i++) {
        var current = window.oneEffects[i];
        if (current.waitForAnimation) { continue }
        var items = Array.from(current.doc.querySelectorAll(current.selector));
        if (items.length > 0) {
            drawHighlights(current.doc, items);
        }
    }
}

<script type="text/javascript">
    (function() {
        var utm = {};
        if (typeof window !== typeof undefined && typeof URLSearchParams !== typeof undefined) {
            var queryParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            queryParams.forEach(function(value, key) {
                if (key.startsWith('utm_')) {
                    utm[ key.split('utm_')[ 1 ] ] = value;
                }
            });
            if (Object.keys(utm).length) {
                window.localStorage.setItem('one_utm', JSON.stringify(utm));
            }
        }
    })();

    
    (function() {
        if (typeof window === typeof undefined) return;

        function readCookie(name) {
            var pairs = (document.cookie || '').split(';');
            for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) {
                var p = pairs[ i ].trim();
                var eq = p.indexOf('=');
                if (eq < 0) continue;
                if (p.slice(0, eq) === name) return decodeURIComponent(p.slice(eq + 1));
            }
            return '';
        }
        function clearCookie(name) {
            document.cookie = name + '=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; SameSite=Lax; Secure';
        }

        var logId = '';
        try {
            logId = (typeof sessionStorage !== typeof undefined && sessionStorage.getItem('op_pw_log_id')) || '';
        } catch (_) {}

        
        if (!logId) {
            logId = readCookie('op_pw_log_id') || '';
            if (logId) {
                try {
                    if (typeof sessionStorage !== typeof undefined) {
                        sessionStorage.setItem('op_pw_log_id', logId);
                    }
                } catch (_) {}
                clearCookie('op_pw_log_id');
            }
        }

        
        if (!logId) return;

        var enterAt = Date.now();
        var sent = false;
        function sendBeacon() {
            if (sent) return;
            sent = true;
            try {
                if (typeof sessionStorage !== typeof undefined) {
                    sessionStorage.removeItem('op_pw_log_id');
                }
            } catch (_) {}
            var timeOnPage = Date.now() - enterAt;
            if (timeOnPage <= 0) return;
            var body = JSON.stringify({ logId: logId, timeOnPage: timeOnPage });
            try {
                if (navigator && typeof navigator.sendBeacon === 'function') {
                    var blob = new Blob([ body ], { type: 'application/json' });
                    navigator.sendBeacon('/_pw/visit', blob);
                    return;
                }
            } catch (_) {}
            try {
                fetch('/_pw/visit', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: body,
                    keepalive: true
                });
            } catch (_) {}
        }
        window.addEventListener('pagehide', sendBeacon);
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', sendBeacon);
    })();
</script>
